### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales, que aún no has compartido, antes de integrarlos: así la historia queda lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir commits que otros tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```

```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history on your own branch before sharing it, like tidying up local commits before a PR. Merge when the branch is shared or you want to keep the real history, since rebasing rewrites commits others may already have.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to recover committed work and roll back anything half-done.
```

```
完成标准：报告修改前后的增量构建耗时，附上实际跑的命令和输出；如果有改动，改动要在你的分支上提交。

不在范围内：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。冷构建的配置如果要动，先说明原因再动。

地点：haswell 上的 hawdb-rs 仓库，沿用你原来的分支。

```

```
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1777 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海，600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">还没记</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">今天的班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有班次，等明早的查询结果。</div>
</main>
<script>
function render(rows) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!rows || !rows.length) { empty.hidden = false; return; }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var under = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.price < 600; });
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = under.map(function (r) { return r.no; }).join('、') + ' 在 600 以下';
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = r.no;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = r.seats || '';
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = '¥' + r.price + (r.price < 600 ? '' : '（超 600）');
    val.style.color = r.price < 600 ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
var stored = ducter.get('trains');
if (stored && stored.then) { stored.then(render); } else { render(stored); }
ducter.onChange(function () {
  var s = ducter.get('trains');
  if (s && s.then) { s.then(render); } else { render(s); }
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座"}]}
```

```
  [partner message] 合计已经放在最上面了，目前是 ¥1,966，记一笔后会自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: changed "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2420 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 · 合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="what">什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="yuan">金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  let t = 0;
  for (const x of xs) {
    t += Number(x.yuan) || 0;
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + Number(x.yuan).toLocaleString("en-US");
    row.append(main, sub, val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  if (!xs.length) {
    const e = document.createElement("div");
    e.className = "k-empty";
    e.textContent = "还没有花销，记了会列在这里";
    list.appendChild(e);
  }
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + t.toLocaleString("en-US");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length + " 笔";
  ducter.summary(xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + t.toLocaleString("en-US"));
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick = () => {
  const what = document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan = parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day = document.getElementById("day").value.trim();
  if (!what || !(yuan > 0)) return;
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  xs.push({ what, yuan, day: day || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) });
  ducter.set("expenses", xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value = "";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value = "";
  document.getElementById("day").value = "";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
  function save() {
    const time = $('#t').value.trim();
    const ml = Number($('#m').value);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(time) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    const [h, m] = time.split(':').map(Number);
    const norm = pad(h) + ':' + pad(m);

    if (editId) {
      const f = feeds.find(x => x.id === editId);
      if (f) { f.time = norm; f.ml = ml; }
    } else {
      feeds.push({ id: String(Date.now()), date: today(), time: norm, ml: ml });
    }
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    resetForm();
    $('#addbox').open = false;
    render();
  }

  function remove() {
    if (!editId) return;
    feeds = feeds.filter(x => x.id !== editId);
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    resetForm();
    $('#addbox').open = false;
    render();
  }

  $('#save').addEventListener('click', save);
  $('#del').addEventListener('click', remove);
  $('#list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (row) openEdit(row.dataset.id);
  });

  feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
  render();
  ducter.onChange(v => { feeds = v || []; render(); });
  setInterval(render, 60000);
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Here's a page you can keep: the four PRs, and you tap each one to Landed as it lands. The count at the top drops as you go.
  [an app card under the message: made "Waiting PRs", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2071 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">PRs not landed yet</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<style>
  .k-row{align-items:center}
  .k-chip[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--d-good);color:var(--d-bg);border-color:var(--d-good)}
  .k-row.done .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
  .k-row-sub{color:var(--d-soft)}
</style>
</main>
<script>
var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
var landed = {};

function render(){
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  var left = 0;
  items.forEach(function(it){
    var on = !!landed[it.id];
    if(!on) left++;
    var row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row" + (on ? " done" : "");
    row.innerHTML =
      '<div class="k-row-main"><div>' + it.title + ' · ' + it.what + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + it.who + '</div></div>' +
      '<button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="' + on + '">' + (on ? "Landed" : "Open") + '</button>';
    row.querySelector("button").onclick = function(){
      landed[it.id] = !landed[it.id];
      save();
      render();
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById("left").textContent = left;
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = left === 0 ? "All landed" : "PRs not landed yet";
  if(window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(left + " waiting, " + (items.length - left) + " landed");
}

function save(){
  if(window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set("landed", landed);
}

if(window.ducter && ducter.get){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function(v){
    if(v && typeof v === "object") landed = v;
    render();
  }, function(){ render(); });
} else {
  render();
}
if(window.ducter && ducter.onChange){
  ducter.onChange(function(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function(v){
      if(v && typeof v === "object"){ landed = v; render(); }
    });
  });
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","who":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","who":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","who":"Draft, not yet asking for review"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
    resetForm();

  };
  $('del').onclick = function(){
    if (editing < 0) return;
    var xs = items();
    xs.splice(editing, 1);
    ducter.set('items', xs);
    resetForm();
    draw();
  };
  $('list').onclick = function(e){
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var i = Number(row.dataset.i), x = items()[i];
    if (!x) return;
    editing = i;
    $('trip').value = x.trip || ''; $('day').value = x.day || ''; $('amt').value = x.amt || ''; $('note').value = x.note || '';
    setCat(x.cat || '其他');
    $('formTitle').textContent = '改这一笔';
    $('del').hidden = false;
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };
  ducter.onChange(draw);
  draw();
})();
```

```
背景：这是对你上一轮 TTL 测试修复的追加，改动仍在原分支（ducter/foo-ttl）上，不要新开分支，也不要合并。

要求：
- 在现有的 TTL 测试里再加一条测试，使用假时钟，把时间推进到正好等于过期时刻（expiry 当刻），断言此时该值已经视为过期（或按 Cache 现有语义，确认边界行为是什么并据此断言），并与“到期前一刻仍有效”的情况形成对照。
- 不要用真实 sleep 或真实计时。
- 完成标准：跑一遍 TTL 相关测试和整套测试，报告测试输出；这条新测试连续跑 20 次都通过。
- 范围外：不要改 Cache 的过期语义本身，除非发现现有边界行为明显是 bug，那就先报告，不要自行改。不要合并，不要推送，不要开 PR。
```
